- **hula-approve**: Approve (step 2 of 2): merge the verified PR and clean everything up — branches, worktrees, and your local main. - **hula-confirm**: Re-validate a plan until it's self-contained and launch-ready (runs automatically after /hula-plan). Use when the user asks to confirm, validate, or refine a plan they've edited. ([NoStackApp/charityBox](https://github.com/NoStackApp/charityBox/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/hula-confirm) · 2 copies)
- **hula-create**: Legacy: create a GitHub issue from the current plan without launching (the modern flow is /hula-plan → launch). Use only when the user explicitly asks for hula-create. - **hula-execute**: Run, schedule, create, or manage execute-actions on the hula-project server. - **hula-fix**: Fix gaps or bugs on a PR branch in an isolated worktree — your working tree is never touched. Use when the user describes a problem to fix on a PR. ([NoStackApp/charityBox](https://github.com/NoStackApp/charityBox/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/hula-fix) · 2 copies)
- **hula-help**: Interactive onboarding and reference guide for hub-launch. - **hula-info**: Peek at a launched run: live logs, PR diff, initial summary, lessons, or the launching session id. Use when the user asks how a run is going or wants to see its output. ([NoStackApp/charityBox](https://github.com/NoStackApp/charityBox/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/hula-info) · 2 copies)
- **hula-launch**: Launch a validated plan: creates the GitHub issue and starts the cloud run that ends in a verified PR (offered automatically after /hula-plan).
